Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the petrochemical structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 291. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 87 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 87. QED.
